- **sonicjs-release-engineer**: - **ALL releases use `npm run version:beta` (`3.x.x-beta.N` → `3.x.x-beta.N+1`) until the user explicitly says "we are out of beta" or "release stable".** NEVER use `version:patch`, `version:minor`, or `version:major` for routine releases — those drop the pre-release suffix and publish a stable version. - **NEVER add `--tag beta` (or alpha/rc) when publishing** — all publishes go to `latest` dist- ([SonicJs-Org/sonicjs](https://github.com/SonicJs-Org/sonicjs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sonicjs-release-engineer.md) · ★1.8k)
